| EasyDental | Brazil, so `blog/pt-br/`. `easydental.com.br`, verified 17 September 2026. **The HTTP 403 that kept this vendor out on 28 August 2026 clears with a browser user agent, so the row is unblocked**; a plain fetch still fails, which is a mechanic and not a reason to skip. The product is "EasyDental Cloud". What they publish and most of this section does not: a prontuário whose odontograma inteligente generates the planos de tratamento e orçamentos from the graphic markings, atestados e receitas with a Memed integration for prescrição digital, a recebimentos module holding particular, convênio and comissões in one plano de contas, a Serasa lookup for restrições financeiras before issuing a boleto, and ANS/TISS conformity with an OdontoPrev/Rede UNNA credential flow in the agenda. A Planos page exists: check whether it carries real figures the run this is written, because that decides whether the "não publica preços" line applies. **Written 19 September 2026. What the run established: the site serves a plain fetch fine with a browser user agent, and the 403 in this row did not recur. "Não publica preços" is wrong here: `/planos/` carries Start R$ 89,00, Standard R$ 219,00 and Clínica R$ 319,00 al mês, 10 % off annually (R$ 961,20 / R$ 2.365,20 / R$ 3.445,20), and the plan gate is the head count, "Até 1", "Até 5" and "Até 10" profissionais, with 5/10/20 Gb of images. The real floor on the top plan is higher than the card: footnote 8 makes a consultoria de implantação of R$ 280,00 mandatory on Clínica, and five recursos opcionais are sold separately with **no price published at all** (assinatura digital, consulta Serasa, pacote de SMS, WhatsApp integrado, espaço extra). Nota fiscal eletrônica, boleto, comissões internas and multiclínica are Clínica-only, the first two "sujeito à análise de viabilidade" and "limitado a 1 (um) CNPJ e 1 (uma) carteira de cobrança". **The page contradicts itself and the trial page contradicts both**: the Standard card lists "Emissão de boletos" under "Recursos do plano Start e mais" while the detailed table marks it absent in Standard, and `recursos.easydental.com.br/teste-gratis` promises "Emissão de boletos e NFe", "Assinatura digital" and "Gerenciamento de comissões" with no plan named. What makes the post is ownership, and it is published by them in three places: *Quem somos* says "Em 2008, o controle da empresa foi adquirido pela OdontoPrev S/A", the 2023 entry names Rede UNNA as a brand that "também faz parte do Grupo Odontoprev", and the privacy policy (7 June 2024) states "A Easy Software faz parte do grupo Odontoprev e, por sua vez, do Grupo Bradesco" and lists sharing with group companies to "consolidar bases cadastrais". So the PMS vendor is controlled by a dental benefits operator whose credential flow it ships. The second centrepiece is the FAQ: "O EasyDental Cloud é 100% dependente de uma conexão internet de boa qualidade" with a 4G chip recommended as redundancy, and access "bloqueado em 10 dias após o vencimento" with the database "definitivamente eliminado" after three months. The same FAQ argues against local databases ("Trata-se de uma falsa sensação"), which is the self-hosting argument met head-on by a competitor and is worth a Queue B answer rather than a rebuttal inside the comparison. Their own founding date is published twice, "desde 1994" on the home page against "desde 1995" on *Quem somos*, and the install figures are 45 mil **dentistas** against 1.200 **clínicas**. Entity: Easy Software S/A, CNPJ 72.995.848/0001-09, in the footer. **`/termos-uso/` publishes the heading and not one clause**, so contract length, fidelidade, aviso prévio, reembolso and SLA are all unpublished, as is the trial length (the trial exists and takes no card). Hosting: the only indication anywhere is a "powered by aws" badge image on `/parceiros/`, verified by opening the PNG; the string AWS appears in no page text and no region or country is named, while the privacy policy admits data that "cruzam a fronteira". The words **periodontograma, ortodontia and API appear on none of the nine pages consulted**, and imaging is storage plus a radiology-clinic partner flow rather than a sensor bridge; no uptime, SLA, ISO 27001 or SOC 2 either, and the post scopes each of those. Rows they win outright: guia TISS with OdontoPrev/Rede UNNA eligibility in the agenda, NFS-e via the prefeitura, boleto with the clinic's own bank, Memed, Serasa, the mobile app they claim works off-line, published support hours (seg-sex 8h-18h, chat and WhatsApp only), managed encrypted daily backups on servers in other cities, and 30 years with Rio 2016 and SESC São Paulo behind them. The honest verdict for a Brazilian clinic that bills convênio is that they fit better today, and the published post says so.** |

Cláusula 4.1 widens the same list (armazenamento extra de imagens e PDFs, Assinaturas Eletrônicas, WhatsApp Business, "qualquer outro serviço") and prices none of it, referring the reader to a Gerente de Contas. | Oralcloud (Globalsoft S.A.) | Portugal. `globalsoft.pt/saude/`, verified 2 October 2026, and this row **corrects a wrong exclusion in this file**: the 23 September run recorded Globalsoft as not added because "Globalsoft's clinic product turned out not to be a dental one". What this section does not otherwise have is **insurer billing as a first-class feature**: the page names ADSE, IASFA, SAD/GNR, PSP and private insurers, which is what a Portuguese clinic with convention patients actually compares on and what our own invoicing has to answer honestly rather than skip. **No price is published**, so the standard line applies with the date consulted. The entity is named, **Globalsoft S.A.**, Rua da Boucinha, 6, 4700-760 Braga; **no NIPC and no AT certification number appear on the page**, and certification has been mandatory in Portugal since 1 January 2011, so say only that no number is published on the pages consulted and check the AT register before writing anything stronger. The lesson for future extensions is the cheaper half of this row: a vendor written off for scope was written off on the wrong page, so when a company sells several verticals, find the vertical's own page before rejecting it. **Written 4 October 2026, and the row understated one thing: Oralcloud is not a standalone dental product, it is the Dentárias tab of the Clicloud health platform.** `/saude/` leads with Clicloud ("um software para clínicas de fisioterapia, dentária e pilates") and has five tabs, Fisioterapia, Hospitais, Dentárias, Pilates e Policlínicas; the Oralcloud copy is **four paragraphs inside the Dentárias tab and that is the entire published product**. `page-sitemap.xml` lists **23 pages** and **fisioterapia and pilates each have a page of their own while the dental product has none**; the contact form's "Área e plataforma" selector offers "Saúde - Clicloud" and no Oralcloud. `oralcloud.pt` resolves to a login screen only, so the app runs but publishes nothing. Confirmed exactly as written: no price anywhere (consultado 4 October 2026), no NIPC, no AT certificate number, the ADSE / IASFA / SAD/GNR / PSP / seguradoras line, and Globalsoft S.A., Rua da Boucinha 6, 4700-760 Braga. **The AT public register was down this entire run**: `consultaProgCertificadosM24.action` returned "Por motivos de ordem técnica não nos é possível responder ao seu pedido" on ten-plus attempts across two sessions and ~15 minutes, so the certification question is still open and the published post asserts neither way. `faturas.portaldasfinancas.gov.pt/painelInicialProdSoftware.action` is **not** a public substitute, it redirects to acesso.gov.pt. The AT's own `Decreto_Lei_28_2019.pdf` **is a scanned PDF, so zlib text extraction returns image data**: read it as pages instead; artigo 4.º is on page 3 and artigo 4.º n.º 3 (the AT must keep the published list) is the cleanest justification for telling a reader to ask for the number. Seven things this row did not know, and they are the post. **The health line was born in physiotherapy**: the institutional page dates it to "Em 1999 ... uma aplicação para o ramo da saúde, mais especificamente para clínicas especializadas na área da medicina física e da reabilitação", and the partnership they advertise is with the Associação Portuguesa de Medicina Física e de Reabilitação. The company history is strong and is theirs to keep: **1992 start in autarquias locais, "Há mais de 32 anos", Lda to S.A. in 2023**, sede in Braga with offices in Lisboa and Ponta Delgada. **"Mais de um milhar de clientes" is company-wide** across autarquias, turismo, faturação and consultoria, not dental clinics, and must be scoped the way the DASI 700+ is in A1; no install-base figure for Oralcloud exists anywhere. **The privacy policy is scoped to the website only** (demo requests, formações, assistência, fórum, parceiros, "tipicamente à identificação de entidades coletivas"), so there is **no artigo 28.º RGPD processing contract, no retention term for clinical records, no hosting location and no subprocessor named**, the Dentasys pattern from A1; there is no terms page at all in the sitemap. **Rows they win outright and that nothing else in A5 has**: faturação to entidades convencionadas with each one's rules, a **patient portal** (App Clicloud's componente do utente, login to book, request session changes and read the full clinical and administrative history), **Globalbooking** online booking wiring the site, Facebook, Instagram and LinkedIn into the agenda with automatic email and SMS confirmation, **Kqueue check-in kiosks** reading the cartão de cidadão or NIF with priority queueing, controlo de stocks, and a published phone (+351 253 300 240/1) with published hours (Seg-Sex 08:30-12:30, 13:30-17:30). Periodontograma, data export, backups and any API appear on **no** page consulted (every "api" hit on `/saude/` is inside fisioterapia). **The product signal worth repeating: with no AT certification and no convenções, Dentalpin is closed out of the Portuguese convention-billing segment entirely**, and the post says so in its own first paragraph |

Cloud ("Sistema 100% em nuvem"), dental-only, with a sister product ControleMÉDICO from the same company. Claims "Mais de 50 mil profissionais", "4000+ Clínicas transformadas", "20+ anos de experiência" and "3+ países", so quote the unit each time, professionals against clinics, as with Prontuário Verde. **It publishes per-chair prices, which nobody else in this section does**: Plano Smart "A partir de 12x de R$ 149,00" per "equipo / cadeira de atendimento" and limited to 2 chairs, and Plano Clinic "A partir de 12x de R$ 220,80" per chair, both "Valor integral parcelado no cartão de crédito", which reads as an annual commitment paid in instalments. Gestão de convênios, the patient app and CRM are Clinic-only. The billing unit is the post: what a 4-chair or 8-chair clinic pays, worked out from their own figures. Also claims "API aberta", "Usuários ilimitados", a fintech arm (Appfin.Net: boletos, PIX parcelado, antecipação de recebíveis), a check-in totem, 3D odontogram and an AI layer (ZaiONe). Entity in the terms of use: APLICATIVO SISTEMAS AVANÇADOS LTDA, CNPJ 05.199.246/0001-58, Paulínia/SP. **Written 9 October 2026. One thing this row had wrong and six it did not know.** **The "API aberta" claim is not sourceable and must not be repeated**: the string "API" appears on no page consulted, including the homepage rendered in a real browser, and there is no developer page. The published post scopes it to "não aparece nas páginas consultadas" and says so of periodontograma too, which is likewise absent everywhere. **The 403 did not reproduce**: plain curl with a browser user agent read every page this run, and `www.controleodonto.com.br/sitemap.xml` (301 to `/wp-sitemap.xml`) enumerates the pages, so absences can be scoped honestly. A browser is still worth it for two things, because the raw HTML misleads: lazily-loaded Elementor sections, and the `/sobre-nos/` timeline, whose year labels sit in a horizontal carousel and pair with their headings by `left` offset, not by document order. **The row had only the annual tariff. The monthly one is published too**, on `/planos/`: Smart "A partir de R$ 164,00 /mês" and Clinic "A partir de R$ 249,00 /mês", both "Pagamento via boleto · fidelidade 12 meses", against the annual "Valor integral parcelado no cartão de crédito". **The install base is published twice and the two do not reconcile**: "Mais de 50 mil profissionais" on the home and `/sobre/` against "Mais de 22.000 profissionais" on `/erp-odontologico/`, a page whose own footer reads "Copyright © ControleODONTO 2024"; the post carries both. **What makes the post is the chair gate, and it is sharper than the row knew**: Smart stops at 2 equipos, so the third chair forces the Clinic rate on all three, taking the per-chair instalment from R$ 149,00 to R$ 220,80 (+48 %) and the monthly bill from R$ 298,00 to R$ 662,40, which more than doubles for one chair. Their own FAQ forbids under-declaring ("Não. Além de não ser permitido...") and defends the model as "precificação justa", and cláusula 3.2.3 fines omitted equipos 100 %, 200 % on reincidência. **The contract is the other half**: cláusula 9.1 publishes a 95 % availability floor with proportional credit (9.2), 8.1 g publishes support 8h-18h on business days with a 4-business-hour response, 6.6 reajusts annually by the greater of IGPM or IPCA, 5.3.1 allows export during the term and 8.2 keeps backups 7 days for judicial orders only; terms are revisão 02 de 19/01/2024. Rows they win outright: gestão de convênios (Clinic only), NFS-e emission, the published SLA, the published support hours, ISO 9001:2015, self-service Excel export at any time ("sem precisar solicitar", plus their own line that "os dados da sua clínica são de sua propriedade"), usuários ilimitados, and the AppFin.Net / marketplace / contabilidade digital ecosystem. `/sobre/` carries the cleaner timeline (2001 Aplicativo.Net, founded by Reinaldo Ferreira; 2007 ControleMÉDICO; 2009 cloud; 2010 ControleODONTO; 2024 Z.AI; 2025 AppFin.Net), so the dental product is 16 years old and the company 25. Migration is "inclusa no valor da implantação" with the implantação price published nowhere, and the route is a patient-register Excel sheet, not the clinical record. ZaiONe is "um serviço adicional nativo", also unpriced. Hosting: the privacy policy says "servidores seguros do próprio ControleODONTO ou em outra tecnologia hospedada internamente" and "eventualmente também podem ser armazenados em nuvem", with no country, region or provider named. They publish two different 0800 numbers on different pages, so quote neither. **Dentalpin appears nowhere on the site**, so there is nothing to answer |

| Dentalis (Ecossistema MV) | Brazil, so `blog/pt-br/`. `dentalis.com.br`, verified 8 October 2026 (**serves a "Verifying your browser" JavaScript page to a plain fetch and to curl with a browser user agent; it cleared on its own in a real browser, no CAPTCHA, and the subpages then fetched normally from inside the page**). Cloud since 2014, dental-only, three products for three segments: DentalSmart (dentistas), DentalFlex (consultórios) and DentalMax (redes de clínicas), plus a clinic-school version. *Quem somos* publishes a timeline: "1990 Lançamento da 1ª versão do programa Dentalis", "2014 Lançamento da versão 100% online e na nuvem", "2020 ... Wallet Digital Dentalis Conta (Bank as a Service)", "2022 +17mil dentistas cadastrados em +1.000 consultórios, clínicas e escolas", and states "atendendo mais de 18.000 usuários" while "Integrando o Ecossistema MV", so the PMS belongs to MV, one of Brazil's large hospital-software groups, which is the ownership angle of the post (as EasyDental and OdontoPrev). **Their own page disagrees with itself**: "34 anos de experiência" against a 1990 first release. **No price on any page consulted** and **no CNPJ on the home, *Quem somos* or privacy-policy pages**, so establish the entity before writing or say exactly that **Written 12 October 2026. Fetch mechanics first: the browser challenge clears on its own in headless Chromium (Playwright, the binary at `/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux/chrome`), and every subpage then reads normally from the same context, so this vendor needs no skip. The fetch tool alone gets only the interstitial. Four corrections to this row.** First, **the *Quem somos* timeline is real but invisible**: 1990, 2012 ("App Dr. Consulta, atualmente iConsulta"), 2014, 2020 and 2022 are all in the document, and every one of those elements computes to `visibility: hidden`, so a visitor never sees them. The published post cites them as page source and says they do not render, rather than presenting them as visible copy. Second, **there is no clinic-school product page**: `/solucoes/dentaluni/` is linked from the home page, is absent from the sitemap and returns "A página não pode ser encontrada". What names the escolas segment is MV's own ecosystem card, not Dentalis. Third, **"no price" and "no CNPJ" are both confirmed and now exhaustive rather than scoped**: `page-sitemap.xml` lists thirteen pages in total, no price, plans or mensalidade page exists among them, and no figure in reais appears in the visible text of any of them; razão social and CNPJ appear on none of home, *Quem somos*, Contato, Termos de Uso or the privacy policy, which publish only the São Paulo address, a phone number and "© 2026 Dentalis Software". Fourth, **the install-base figure is published two different ways**: *Quem somos* says "atendendo mais de 18.000 usuários em todo o Brasil" and the DentalFlex page says "Junte-se a mais de 18.000 clientes", which is users in one place and customers in the other. The home page's "Dentalis em números" section renders with no figure at all, the power.dent trap, so nothing was taken from it. Four more things the row did not know, and they are what makes the post. **The comparison is embedded banking, not features**: "Recursos que só a Dentalis oferece" is "Conta digital gratuita para fazer pagamentos e emitir boletos direto em nosso software", DentalFlex publishes "boletos com conciliação automática" plus patient credit, and DentalSmart names the partners, "integrado à maquineta Stone para cartão de crédito e meios de pagamentos digitais FitBank". No open-source PMS competes with that and the post concedes it up front. **The Termos de Uso page is published and empty**, a heading and nothing else, so contract length, fidelidade, cancellation terms and system requirements cannot be compared at all, in either direction. **The privacy policy is the most quotable page on the site, both ways**: it authorises Dentalis to "armazenar, processar usar, transmitir ou disponibilizar a terceiros informações obtidas durante o uso da plataforma" and to use all collected information for "o estabelecimento de uma base de dados de propriedade intelectual do Dentalis", and it is also better than the market average in publishing numeric retention periods (registration data "por até 1 ano após encerramento", audit logs "1 ano"), a 15-working-day LGPD response deadline and an explicit portability right. It says data sits "nos servidores do Dentalis" and **names no country, provider or datacentre**, so that row is scoped. **The only occurrence of "API" anywhere on the site is in the privacy policy, about cookies and web beacons**, so no product API is documented; periodontograma and parodontograma appear on none of the thirteen pages, while the odontograma is described in detail. **MV is the one acceptable second source and it is the parent's own site**: `mv.com.br/ecossistema` publishes "as 13 empresas do Ecossistema liderado pela MV" and a Dentalis card reading "Empresa focada na gestão completa para o segmento odontológico, no modelo SAAS, para clínicas, escolas e consultórios", linking to dentalis.com.br. Use that, not the acquisition press: the 2023 MV-buys-Dentalis story, the "6 mil consultórios" figure and the acquisition date exist only in trade publications and were deliberately not reused. Post lives at `blog/pt-br/dentalpin-vs-dentalis.md`.** |

Second, **the privacy policy publishes the data-location answer and it is the row we win**: "Fornecedores globais podem processar dados fora do Brasil", with no hosting provider, no country, no data centre, no backup frequency, no encryption claim and no certification, only "São adotados controles técnicos e administrativos compatíveis com o serviço"; the only third parties named are Google Ads, WhatsApp/Meta and Kiwify. Third, **the AI add-on is named two ways on their own page**, Sofia IA in the product copy and "Kiwify IA na licença: 30 dias grátis, depois R$ 79/mês (opcional)" in the pricing block, where Kiwify is also their payment processor: quote both and do not resolve it. Fourth, **periodontograma, nota fiscal/NFS-e and an API of their own appear on none of the pages consulted** (the only API named is Meta's, for WhatsApp), while estoque and "Controle de estoque" do appear, so the absences are scoped rather than asserted. | VeVi Clinic (VeVi Systems S.L.) | Portugal. `veviclinic.com`, verified 8 October 2026 (**the site defaults to Spain and Spanish. The Portuguese version is set by PATCH `/web/country?country=PT` and `/web/lang?lang=pt` with the page's CSRF token in a cookie jar. A plain GET of `?lang=pt` returns an error JSON**). A Spanish dental-only cloud vendor, A Coruña, "© 2017-2026 VEVI Systems S.L.", registered in the Registro Mercantil de A Coruña, Tomo 3.499, Folio 195, Hoja C-50.675. **It is not in A1**, and it is queued here because its Portugal page is specific to Portugal: "Programa certificado pela AT. Certificado Nº 3086", and a Portugal phone line, +351 300 527 545. The AT register matches: "VEVI CLINIC", versão PT20250910, certificado n.º **3086**, VEVI SYSTEMS S L, Certificado, **2026-03-17**, the second-newest health certificate on the list. **Publishes prices**: Mini €19,9/mês, Estândar €29,9/mês (up to 5 users), Premium €59,9/mês (up to 15 users), Enterprise "Consultar" (read API, own server and domain). The page consulted does not say whether IVA is included, so do not state it either way. Claims ISO 27001, hourly backups and odontograma plus periodontograma. The angle no other A5 row has is the **lab link**: its sister product VeVi Dental is laboratory software (AT cert. 2885, 2021-01-15), and their page says "Integração com laboratórios e Vevi Dental, disponível em todos os planos" **Written 14 October 2026. Two corrections to this row and seven things it did not know.** The fetch mechanic is a Rails app, not Odoo: `veviclinic.com` 301s to `www.veviclinic.com`, then `PATCH /web/country?country=PT` and `PATCH /web/lang?lang=pt` with the page's `csrf-token` meta value sent as `X-CSRF-Token` and a cookie jar. A plain GET returns `{"error":"La dirección introducida no es correcta."}`. All twelve content pages then read cleanly. **Correction 1: "The page consulted does not say whether IVA is included" is wrong.** The `/web/plans` tariff carries footnote (1), "Impostos não incluídas (se aplicável)", so the four figures are net. The tariff itself is confirmed exactly. **Correction 2: "odontograma plus periodontograma" is wrong as a flat claim, and that is what makes the post.** The plan table marks **periodontograma unavailable in mini and estándar** and available only in premium and enterprise, while the home page presents it inside the patient module next to the odontograma and the CAD/CAM files: both are published, carry both. Gestão de mútuas is gated the same way. What the row did not know. **mini is a single-user plan** ("Múltiplos utentes" unavailable; estándar Até 5, premium Até 15) **and has no reports at all**, not even the basic ones; email sending to patients, exportação a contabilidade, supplier management, payment plans, document templates and staff time logging all start at premium. Add-ons sit outside every plan including enterprise: WhatsApp 6 €/mês, external agenda integration 6 €/mês, patient mobile app 12 €/mês, PWA 6 €/mês, remote diagnosis 12 €/mês (premium only). So a practice wanting periodontograma plus WhatsApp is at 59,9 + 6 = **65,9 €/mês net**, labelled as arithmetic on their figures. **The ISO 27001 claim is verifiable and is theirs, not a data centre's**: the linked PDF is certificate **SI-0563/23** from LGAI Technological Center, S.A. (Applus+) to VEVI SYSTEMS, S.L., ISO/IEC 27001:2022, scope "Consultoría, desarrollo y soporte de software, en modalidad SaaS", SoA 11/03/2026 v04, initial issue 28/04/2023, valid 28/04/2026 to 27/04/2029. Concede it generously; it is the opposite of the Dentasys and Odontosys pattern. **Hosting is named down to the country**, AWS with "atualmente a Amazon mantém os seus servidores na Irlanda", which no earlier A5 Portuguese comparison achieves. **Backups are published two ways and cancellation twice over**: home page "cópias de segurança a cada hora" / "horárias" against security page and FAQ "cópias de segurança diárias"; FAQ "Apenas lhe serão faturados os dias que usufruiu" and cancel-anytime against clause 4.2's fifteen days' notice on tacit renewal and 4.3's no refund of prepaid amounts. Carry both of each. **The strongest finding is the contract, and it is the whole post's second half**: the Condições gerais, Aviso legal and Política de privacidade of the Portuguese site are published **only in Castilian**, built on the Spanish Ley 34/2002, naming VEVI SYSTEMS, S.L., NIF B70388947; clause 12 gives Spanish law, "Los Tribunales Españoles serán los únicos competentes" and the A Coruña courts "con expresa renuncia a cualquier otro fuero", and clause 9.1 routes data-protection complaints to the **AEPD, not the CNPD**. **Clause 9.2 cuts both ways and must be carried whole**: it is a real article 28 RGPD processing contract (art. 32 measures, a DPIA, sub-processor notification, "Vevi no almacenará los datos del cliente en países no pertenecientes a la UE", thirty days and return "en un formato estructurado", breach notification), which most of A5 does not publish, and the same clause reserves access to "tratamientos odontológicos, citas, facturación, gestión de almacen y pedidos" "incluso a través de aplicaciones de inteligencia artificial, con el fin de que VEVI pueda realizar informes estadísticos y/o tareas de investigación y desarrollo", against the security page's "nem os utilizamos para qualquer outro fim alheio ao próprio funcionamento da Vevi Clinic". State both and ask how they reconcile; never adjectivise it. The security page also invokes the **repealed Diretiva 95/46/CE** and the Spanish AEPD's cloud recommendations, while the contract cites Regulation (UE) 2016/679 correctly. **SPMS, PEM and prescrição eletrónica appear on none of the twelve pages consulted** (they publish "Prescrição digital", "Receitas médicas" and "Importação de receitas em XML", which is not the SPMS platform): scope it as an absence on the pages consulted, the way the kiCLINIC post does. **No numeric install-base figure anywhere**, only "Milhares de clientes e utilizadores ativos em diferentes países". **The AT register answers a plain fetch with a browser user agent and is latin-1, not UTF-8** (decode it as latin-1 or the parse dies); consulted 14 October 2026, last updated 2026-09-14, **3.103 entries of which 3.090 "Certificado"**, and it confirms VEVI CLINIC PT20250910 n.º 3086 estado Certificado 2026-03-17, the 18th newest entry by date. It also lists a **third product this row did not know, CORUS LINK, PT20210211, n.º 2897, 2021-04-09**, alongside VEVI DENTAL n.º 2885: relevant because the contract and the módulos page both name a "Care Channel" integration. Dentalpin is in none of the 3.103. Rows they win outright: the AT certificate, the ISO certificate, three published prices, the named hosting country, the article 28 clause, free migration and training with "Sem custos iniciais", a 30-day trial, "Suporte 12 horas por dia" on a Portuguese line (+351 300 527 545), the Vevi Dental lab link in every plan, and 2017 against our 2026. One more thing that is fact and quotation only: the localisation is half-done on their own Portuguese pages, the módulos page reads in native European Portuguese while the plan table's "Automatizaciones e IA" block is in Castilian, the plan is spelled "estándar", the yes/no images carry `alt="Sí"`/`alt="No"`, and one feature row reads "Modelo 347 e registro de IRPF/IVA com provedores", both Spanish tax instruments. Handle it as the FELG Dent row says: no adjectives, which is what makes it land. Post lives at `blog/pt/dentalpin-vs-vevi-clinic.md`. |

The module says "Up to 20 Users" and "2 specialties without customization". **Publishes prices, plus IVA**, as a monthly fee on a **3-year commitment**: Start-UP 8,90 €/mês (2 users, "Limitado a 100 clientes"), Starter 15,90 €/mês (5 users), Pro 28,00 €/mês (20 users), Advanced 44,00 €/mês (30 users). Annual commitment costs more (12,90 / 22,00 / 38,00 / 54,00 €/mês) and month-to-month more still (Start-UP 19,90 €/mês). The page does not make clear whether the dental module is included in these plans or priced separately, so check before writing. **The certification needs care, because the two sources disagree.** Their own terms say the software is "certified by the AT with number 3016" and name the contracting entity as **Galáxia D'inteligência Lda, NIPC 518 691 543, Rua do Campo Alegre 1517 C16, 4150-182 Porto**. The AT register shows certificate n.º **3016**, "TUGSiS", versão 0.0.1, but the produtor is **DREAMKANGAROO LDA** (2023-12-22). State both exactly and draw no conclusion. The about page still shows **template placeholder testimonials** ("Nome da pessoa / Cargo / Empresa"), so there is no customer evidence to cite |

The pt-PT dental page is real and detailed: FDI-numbered odontograma with faces, an attachment for the "Avaliação Periodontal", treatment plans and quotes, a patient portal and a doctor app. It publishes a **per-user price**, "Desde 10€/utilizador por mês", the only per-seat tariff in this section. It cites a Portuguese regulatory basis no other row names: MCDT requisition "Autorizado nos termos da Portaria n.º 263/2023, de 17 de agosto", and "Solução de confiança para clínicas que operam no âmbito do SNS". **The entity is UK-registered**: "© MASTERINSOFT - Intelligence in Software and Computing Engineering, Ltd", "Head Office – UK" (+44 20 4577 0206), with a "Regional Office – Portugal" on +351 221 155 908. That is a Porto number, but no Portuguese company or NIPC is published. Establish who contracts, the way the DenPro row does with its LLC. **Nothing on the page claims AT certification for invoicing, and no Masterinsoft entry appears in the AT register** (checked 8 October 2026). State that as a register result with its date, never as an accusation. **Written 18 October 2026, and the row needed four corrections.** First, **the two Portarias were swapped**: the dental page attributes "Autorizado no Âmbito da Portaria nº 126/2018, de 8 de maio" to the MCDT requisition and "Autorizado nos termos da Portaria n.º 263/2023, de 17 de agosto" to prescription (PEM), not the reverse. Second, **"nothing on the page claims AT certification" is wrong and must not be reused**: the page lists "Emissão de Faturas Eletrónicas (Conformidade com AT)" and "Totalmente conforme com as regras portuguesas de faturação eletrónica (SAF-T PT, e-Fatura)". What is absent is a certificate number, not the claim. Third, **the "Desde 10€/utilizador por mês" is not the tariff**: `/pt/precos/` publishes five organisation-level plans in English, START €59, GROW €109, PRO €159, PREMIUM €209 and ELITE €259 per month with 5, 10, 15, 20 and 25 users included, additional users at €20, €19, €17, €15 and €14, "Billed monthly · 12-month commitment", ENTERPRISE on request, no VAT wording and no trial. The €10 matches no plan: entry is €59/month (€11.80/user) and the figure is only reached by dividing ELITE (€259 ÷ 25 = €10.36), so it needs 25 users. Implementation is priced separately, "Software subscription and implementation are separate". Fourth, **who contracts is answerable and the answer is the post**: the entity is real and filing, Companies House **14887558, MASTERINSOFT - INTELLIGENCE IN SOFTWARE AND COMPUTING ENGINEERING LTD, active, private limited, incorporated 23 May 2023**, 38 Wilcox Road, London SW8 2UX, accounts to 31 May 2025, confirmation statement 23 April 2026, so this is **not** the Dentra failure mode. But no Portuguese entity, NIPC or address appears anywhere on the site, `/legal-notice/` and `/terms-of-service/` are **unfilled "Lorem ipsum" template text**, and the one terms document with real content, `/termos-e-condicoes/`, names **"PANTHER Ambition, LDA . (adiante "PANTHER")"** as licensor throughout and **"Secularfragment, Lda."** in its e-invoicing sections, with a "[funcionalidade]" placeholder, while still pointing prices at "www.masterinsoft.com": an adapted lift, not a page served by accident. That document is where the contract shape lives (auto-renewal, opposition "com pelo menos 25 dias de antecedência", advance direct debit, access to software and stored data ceasing "automaticamente e sem necessidade da PANTHER proceder a qualquer aviso prévio", data kept 30 days after termination then destroyed with no format named) and its data-protection clause rests on **Lei n.º 67/98**, the pre-RGPD law, against the page's "Conformidade Total com o RGPD por Conceção". Four more things the row did not know. The scope is wider than it says: thirteen sectors in the solutions menu, down to hotelaria, turismo and aluguer. Two of their own pages disagree: the dental page claims "Ambiente cloud certificado ISO 27001 / ISO 27701" and "Instalação on-premises opcional para grandes instituições", while `/pt/tecnologia-e-arquitetura/` names no ISO and no on-premise, only "cloud privada dedicada", and publishes the data location as "regiões otimizadas no Reino Unido, União Europeia e Estados Unidos", which is not the dental page's "infraestrutura europeia". There is **no install-base figure anywhere**, and the partner page's reference organisations (LNEC, IT, NOVA Medical School, KOPKE GROUP, IHMT) include **no dental clinic**; it also states "Os serviços profissionais prestados pelo Partner constituem uma relação comercial direta entre o Partner e o cliente", so implementation may be a second contract with a second counterparty. **The AT register of certified invoicing programs is reachable, and the
working address matters.** The `dentalpin-vs-tugsis` post of 17 October 2026
recorded that it could not be consulted at
`portaldasfinancas.gov.pt/pt/consultaProgCertificadosM24.action`. On
18 October 2026 the full list came back in one 732 kB response from
**`https://www.portaldasfinancas.gov.pt/pt/Out/consultaProgCertificadosM24.action`**
(note the `/Out/` segment) with a browser user agent and no login: about 3.098
rows with state "Certificado", state dates to 12 October 2026, columns Nome do
Programa, Versão, Nome do Produtor, N.º Certificado, Estado and Data Estado, and
no pagination. Two sibling addresses are soft 404s that answer HTTP 200 with "O
pedido efetuado corresponde a uma página que não existe", which is how this
reads as unreachable: `faturas.portaldasfinancas.gov.pt/consultarProgramasCertificados.action`
and the same path without `/Out/`. Any Portuguese row that turns on an
invoicing claim should check this list and quote the result with its date, for
the vendor and for us: **Dentalpin has no entry either**, and saying so is what
keeps the row fair.

One thing that check turned up for an already published post, to fold into
`dentalpin-vs-tugsis` on its next re-verification rather than into a new file:
certificate **3016 is registered in the AT list as program "TUGSiS" version
0.0.1 under the producer "DREAMKANGAROO LDA"**, not under Galáxia
D'inteligência Lda, which is the entity the Tugsis terms name. The number the
vendor publishes is right; the registered producer is a different company, and
that is a question for them rather than a correction to make silently.

### A6 · Germany — German posts rank here

Unblocked: the site publishes in German since July 2026. **These are
written in German only**, and one post covers Germany, Austria and
German-speaking Switzerland.

Every row below was reached at the vendor's own domain on 30 July 2026.
None of them published a price there except ivoris, which turned out to
publish a full tariff PDF; see that row. The claims in the "Why" column
are what the vendor says about itself, or a market figure that still has
to be re-sourced the run the post is written.

| Target | Slug | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Dampsoft (DS-Win / DS4) | `dentalpin-vs-dampsoft` | `dampsoft.de`. The incumbent, family-owned since 1986. Publishes two products on its own site: DS-WIN on-premise and DS4 web-based, which is our cloud-versus-your-server argument stated by them. **The "market leader" line comes from third parties, so either source it from a KZBV figure or drop it.** |
| CGM Z1.PRO (CompuGroup Medical) | `dentalpin-vs-cgm-z1` | `cgm.com/deu_de`. Sells Z1.PRO for practices and HIGHDENT PLUS for university clinics and group practices. Its own page says a standard price is "not possible" because every practice is individual, which is the cleanest published quote on pricing opacity we have in any market |
| charly (solutio) | `dentalpin-vs-charly` | `solutio.de`, solutio GmbH & Co. KG. Sells through `solutioshop.de` and offers a free demo. **The domain `charly.de` does not resolve, always link solutio.de** |
| EVIDENT | `dentalpin-vs-evident` | `evident.de`, EVIDENT GmbH. Claims over 50 years and a modular build that "grows with your requirements", so the module argument has to be made on substance, not on the word |
| ivoris (Computer konkret AG) | `dentalpin-vs-ivoris` | `ivoris.de`. Splits into ivoris dent, ortho and clinic. A per-speciality split is a real difference from one product with modules. **Checked 13 August 2026: the "none of them published a price" line above is wrong for this row. `ivoris.de/preise/` redirects to a full price list PDF (`2026-7-preisliste.pdf`, valid 1.7. to 31.8.2026) with a monthly rent for every product, module and service. It is the only German vendor in A6 that does, and the published post is built around it.** |
| LinuDent (PHARMATECHNIK) | `dentalpin-vs-linudent` | `linudent.de`. Verify what it is before writing: PHARMATECHNIK's roots are pharmacy software, and the size of the dental line is the first thing to establish |
| DENSoffice (DENS GmbH) | `dentalpin-vs-densoffice` | `zahnarztsoftware.de` (yes, DENS GmbH operates that generic domain), verified 28 August 2026. **Checked 3 September 2026: three corrections to this row. DENSvisuell is not patient education, it is the "Detailbefundung mittels moderner Zahngrafik", i.e. the charting module; patient education is DENS.marketing. The user count is on their own site after all: "Mittlerweile verwenden über 2000 Zahnarztpraxen in ganz Deutschland das leicht erlernbare und effektive Praxisverwaltungsprogramm DENSoffice" on `/gute-gruende-fuer-dens/`. And "no published prices" is wrong here: there is no price page, but the home page links three PDFs, and the Sonderangebot one is an order form quoting 9.900 € Aktionspreis (regulär 13.900 €) plus 290 €/Monat Lizenzgebühr, with a full module list. The two others price support and training by the hour. What is genuinely absent is any system requirements page, so Windows-only cannot be stated from their site; the published post says so rather than guessing.** |
| dios ZX (Spitta GmbH) | `dentalpin-vs-dios-zx` | `dios.de`, a brand of Spitta GmbH, verified 28 August 2026. Sells dios ZX for billing and practice management; its historic strength is hygiene documentation (dios MP), so establish the PMS's real weight before writing |
| teemer (ARZ.dent GmbH) | `dentalpin-vs-teemer` | `teemer.de`, verified 28 August 2026. 100% cloud, from anamnesis to billing, part of the ARZ Haan group. **Checked 5 September 2026: two things this row had wrong and three it did not know. "No published install base" is wrong: the FAQ answers "Wieviele Anwender hat die teemer Praxissoftware mittlerweile?" with "Wir sind stolz seit geraumer Zeit die 500er-Marke geknackt zu haben", and that is users, not practices. "Young product" is wrong too: the same FAQ says "Die teemer Praxissoftware ist bereits seit 2014 als erste cloudbasierte Lösung bei der KZBV zugelassen", which is the strongest single line any German vendor in A6 publishes and the thing the post has to concede up front. `/faq/` is where this vendor puts everything worth having, not the product pages. It is not quite pure cloud: a "leistungsfähiger PC" in the practice runs their healthconnect encryption layer, and "Klardaten verlassen niemals die Praxis". No euro figure anywhere, but the formula is published (one-off setup, then monthly per Behandler excluding Prophylaxe and per connected Standort), and so is the commitment: two years, auto-renewing by one, six months' notice, which is two and a half years of lead time and is what makes the post. Also stated on their own pages: TI complete, VDDS Media plus Sidexis (Slida), CTAPI, VDDS-RZ, "nicht modular aufgebaut" but Online-Terminbuchung sold as an "(Add-on)", MKG "aktuell noch nicht inkludiert", and a published downtime of 0,213 %. The honest verdict for a German Kassenpraxis is that teemer fits better today, and the published post says so.** |

"No published price" is only half right: there is no figure for either ZaWin kaufen or ZaWin mieten ("Ein Preis pro Monat"), but three Franken figures are published, and none of them is the normal price: the Neustarter "5 Franken Modell" at 5 CHF pro Patient, 1 CHF to take over the predecessor's ZaWin on a practice handover, and a Wartungsvertrag Plus at "nur 1 Franken pro Tag" (news, 4 December 2024). What makes the post is the Swiss tariff: their news of 30 August 2024 explains the SSO Leistungspakete Plus (LP+) in force from 1 January 2025 and that invoices and dossiers to UV/MV/IV may be submitted "ausschliesslich elektronisch", transition to 30 June 2025; their news of 28 August 2026 announces that the ZAS moves IV invoice transmission from MediData to Swisscom's curaLINE on 1 January 2027 and that ZaWin will support it. Four more things the row did not know. The history page dates the company to 1981 and a 25% market share to 1995, so "40+ years" is the company, and the 15.000 is people, not practices (2022 said "über 10.000 Praxismitarbeiter"). Röntgen runs over VDDS and SLIDA, the same standards as A6's German vendors, and caries assessment on bitewings is done with the Swiss firm Nostic. A dedicated Parostatus is named only for DHWin, the product for self-employed Dentalhygienikerinnen, not for the main product. And the Datenschutzerklärung's "Ihre Personendaten werden in der Schweiz gespeichert" plus "Microsoft speichert unsere Daten ausschliesslich in der Schweiz" covers the data Martin Engineering processes about customers and site visitors; nothing on the pages consulted says where ZaWin Cloud patient data sits, and the published post says so rather than reusing that line. No system requirements, no contract length and no API documentation anywhere, all three scoped to "die konsultierten Seiten". The honest verdict for a Swiss practice billing UV/MV/IV is that they fit better today, and the published post says so.** |

| OrisDent Q (OrisLine) | `dentalpin-vs-orisdent` | `orisline.com`. Publishes a whole product family (OrisDent Q, the cloud OrisDent Air, OrisLab Q for labs). A suite sold as one is the closest thing Italy has to our module argument, so meet it head-on |
| GipoDental (GIPO) | `dentalpin-vs-gipodental` | `gipo.it`. **Checked 6 August 2026: the pricing pages exist (`/prezzi`, `/prezzi-gipodental`) and list what each plan contains, but carry no figure, only "Prezzo calcolato individualmente". So the "non pubblica i prezzi" line holds, with that precision.** Ownership: the site's legal entity is Docplanner Italy Srl, and the same group owns MioDottore, which GipoDental integrates with. Verified from their own site |
| AlfaDocs | `dentalpin-vs-alfadocs` | `alfadocs.com`. Cloud-first, positioned around the dentist-patient relationship rather than the back office. A different pitch from ours, which makes "scegli AlfaDocs se" the section that carries the post |
| Appuntoo | `dentalpin-vs-appuntoo` | `appuntoo.com`. Aims at small and medium practices and markets a free tier, so it is the one Italian row where free-versus-free has to be compared carefully and fairly |
| UNO (Dental Trey s.r.l.) | `dentalpin-vs-uno` | `dentaltrey-uno.it`, verified 28 August 2026. 30+ years of dental software, claims 8.000 professionals, cloud ("MyBusiness Cloud"). Dental Trey is first a supplies distributor; the software division is the target. The product is UNO: no product named "Evolution" exists on its site. **Checked 2 September 2026: five things this row did not know. The site sits behind a SiteGround captcha (`sg-captcha: challenge`, HTTP 202 with a 169-byte refresh stub) that defeats a plain fetch: retry with a cookie jar until a 200 comes back, it does within a handful of tries. It publishes both a licence price and canoni, so the "none of them published a price" line above is wrong for this row: licence "a partire da € 1.900 per 3 posti di lavoro" plus Start-Up da € 26,5/mese (max 4 posti), Full da € 76/mese and Full Digital da € 130/mese (posti illimitati), with 12-month financing or a 36-month BNP leasing. The asterisk in their plan table means "Attivabile come add on", and both "Fatturazione elettronica" and "Invio telematico Tessera Sanitaria" carry it on all three plans, so neither Italian obligation is included in any plan: that is what makes the post. `MyBusiness Cloud` is a cloud KPI layer over a product licensed per `posto di lavoro`, not a cloud PMS, so do not write it as cloud-native. And ownership is stated on their own Chi Siamo page, not inferred: "Nel 2015 Dental Trey si è rafforzata grazie all'entrata nel gruppo Henry Schein Inc." and the informatica division "è parte del gruppo internazionale Henry Schein ONE", the same group as Dentrix, Dentally and Software of Excellence in A3.** |

Aggregators attribute it to Zucchetti while its own site declares Quaderno Elettronico S.r.l.: state ownership only as its own site does. **Checked 3 September 2026: the ownership guard held, no group is named anywhere on their own pages, so nothing was repeated from elsewhere. Five things the row did not know. It publishes prices, so the "none of them published a price" line above is wrong here too: acquisto "a partire da 750€" (with the first fiscal year of fatturazione elettronica up to 1000 documents, Sistema TS, 6 hours of consulenza and 100 SMS credits) plus assistenza "a partire da 200€ /anno" or "a partire da € 50 /mese", two figures that do not reconcile and are both "a partire da". The Italian compliance set is their strong side and is complete: Sistema TS, SDI with ten-year conservazione, and a dedicated Referto FSE 2.0 service whose page states the 31 March 2026 obligation. "Quaderno Online" is remote access to the installed program on their server, not a browser-native cloud PMS: do not write it as cloud. MIA is a paid-looking AI telephone assistant with no published price, like most of the 39 servizi. And no page consulted publishes system requirements, so Windows-only cannot be stated from their site; the published post says so rather than guessing.** |
| DentalOpera (MedicalOpera srl) | `dentalpin-vs-dentalopera` | `dentalopera.it`, verified 28 August 2026. Founded 2017, MedicalOpera srl, Cervignano del Friuli (UD). **Publishes prices: da 88 €/mese on its home page and on `/prezzi/`, so the usual "non pubblica i prezzi" line is wrong here**. **Checked 4 September 2026: two corrections to this row. "100% cloud" is not supported by their own site: the word "cloud" appears on none of the pages consulted, there are no system requirements and no hosting or data location anywhere, so the published post describes it as an account-based gestionale (login on `gest.dentalopera.it`) rather than asserting an architecture the vendor does not state. And the 88 € is a floor, not a price: the same page carries "Nessun modulo nascosto, nessun costo extra per utente" above a MODULI AGGIUNTIVI block of eight unpriced modules, two of which are "Poltrone operative" and "Sedi", and closes with "non abbiamo un listino fisso". Three more things the row did not know. Sistema TS is covered ("Sistema MEF TS: Invio telematico dei dati al Sistema Tessera Sanitaria e MEF") but sits in the same feature list as gestione magazzino, which `/prezzi/` sells as a paid add-on, so whether TS filing is inside the 88 € cannot be told from their pages: the post states both and cites each. Fatturazione elettronica illimitata *is* unambiguously in the base price and FSE is unambiguously an add-on. Clinical depth is real (odontogramma grafico, cartella parodontale, cartella ortodontica, prestazioni per arcata, gestione per riuniti), the only install-base figure is "Già scelto da 600+ studi" on `/agenda-governata/`, onboarding is free, and cancellation is "senza necessità di preavviso".** |
| Dentus (jErgoSoft Srl) | `dentalpin-vs-dentus` | `dentus.it`, verified 28 August 2026. Desktop since 2000, claims 15.000+ clients. **Publishes prices: 33,20-135 €/mese (`/it/prezzi-software-dentale/`)**. **Checked 5 September 2026: the price range is right, and five things the row did not know. All four plans (Facile 41,50 €, Standard 62,50 €, Premium 121 €, Elite 135 €, each 20% less billed annually) carry the word "Monoutenza", and "Collegamento in rete" is a separate module priced nowhere on the site ("la versione limitata a 4 computer, a 8 e licenze aggiuntive"), so a practice with more than one workstation cannot compute its cost from the published tariff: that is what makes the post. The Premium plan does not list the Modulo Parodontale that the cheaper Standard includes, and Modulo MEF730 (the Sistema TS filing) appears only on Premium and Elite. The agenda is itself an add-on module and is absent from the base plan. Fatturazione elettronica is described as a full service ("Facciamo tutto noi: Ricevimento, Spedizione e Conservazione") but appears in no plan row, so whether it is included cannot be told from their pages; the published post says so. And the deployment story is unusually close to ours and was conceded in full: published requirements are "Windows™ 11, 10, 8, 7, XP oppure con MacOsX™ oppure con Linux" via Java, the database goes "Sul tuo PC della rete locale, o sul tuo NAS o sul sito", and the home page states "I Dati che introduci sono sempre di tua proprietà". No VAT note and no contract length anywhere. The word odontogramma appears nowhere; they write "schema dentale". Two more they publish and nobody else does: the ASL di Torino "progetto Castoro" from 2005, and a cartella parodontale "sviluppata con esperti della Dental School di Torino" on 4 or 6 sites.** |
| FELG Dent | `dentalpin-vs-felg-dent-italia` | `felgdent.com/it/`, verified 17 September 2026. The "flaky Italian product site" note below is stale: it fetches cleanly and has its own Italian pages and a `/it/listino/`. The publisher is **Polish**, FELG Software Sp. z o.o., selling into Italy: state the origin plainly rather than leaving it for the reader to find, the way the Aerona row does for its French owner, and do not treat it as a weakness. Claims "Software cloud per il tuo studio dentistico", "oltre 16.000 dentisti" and "Oltre 4000 studi si sono affidati a noi" (**the 16.000 is dentists and hygienists, not studi**, per their own Polish wording), "oltre 10 anni di esperienza", ISO 27001, and "30 giorni di prova, zero obblighi". Check whether `/it/listino/` carries euro figures the run this is written. **What decides this post is Italian compliance**: establish from their Italian pages whether fatturazione elettronica and the Sistema Tessera Sanitaria filing are covered, because the same product's Polish site leads on KSeF and NFZ, which are not Italian obligations, and a feature list translated across a border is exactly what this section has caught before. **Do not give this post the same filename as the A8 FELG Dent row**: posts sharing a filename across directories are emitted as hreflang alternates of each other, and these are two different posts for two different markets, not translations. **Written 20 September 2026. What the run established: every Italian page fetches cleanly with a browser user agent, the stale-note in this row was right. `/it/listino/` carries euro figures, so the "check whether" is settled: SOLO 29 €, START 39 €, FLEX 59 €, MAX 79 €, all "canone mensile netto*" where the asterisk is defined as "*calcolo delle media mensile". The page offers four durations as tabs (Mensile, Annuale, Biennale, Triennale) but only one set of figures is in the served HTML and the others need JavaScript, so **which duration the 29 € belongs to cannot be determined** and the post says so rather than guessing. That matters because the licence agreement bars early exit on prepaid periods ("senza possibilità di un recesso anticipato"), against 30 days' notice on the monthly. The unit is per medico o igienista, not per riunito. The Italian compliance question this row asked is answered and the answer is split: **Sistema TS yes**, quoted from the home page ("FELG Dent consente di inviare le fatture direttamente al Sistema TS, oppure di generare i file — in diversi formati — per lo studio di contabilità"), **SDI no** (the words SDI, Sistema di Interscambio, fattura elettronica and fatturazione elettronica appear on no page consulted, and nothing addresses conservazione a norma). What makes the post is that the localisation is half-done and it is visible on their own pages: three of the thirteen sections on `/it/funzioni/` are in Polish and describe Polish obligations (Rejestr P1, e-Recepty, e-Skierowania, eWUŚ, Rozliczenia NFZ, Przedpłaty za wizyty), the Italian listino sells two configurations priced in złoty ("NFZ Konfiguracja PLN 500", "Certyfikaty P1 konfiguracja PLN 179"), "P1" is listed among the modules every subscription includes, the Italian home page repeats its departments block in Polish and carries "Dodaj tu swój tekst nagłówka" plus Lorem ipsum, four of five client stories on `/it/funzioni/` are Lorem ipsum, and the English API page still shows the template line "No fake quotes or photos!". Handle that as fact and quotation only, never as mockery: the no-adjectives rule is what makes it land. Two things to concede generously, because they are real and documented. The Italian connection is founding, not marketing: `chisiamo` dates the project to 2014 and credits "Guido Pasquantonio, dentista e docente di odontoiatria presso l'Università degli Studi di Roma Tor Vergata", with "circa trenta appassionati tra Polonia e Italia", market launch 2018, plus Italian LinkedIn/Facebook/Instagram/YouTube channels and a Milan phone line, +39 02 21119068. And the parodontal module is deep and documented in Italian: eight parameters per tooth, vestibular versus lingual/palatal, mean CAL, mean pocket depth, plaque and BOP percentages, comparison against previous exams. Entity published plainly on `/it/contatti/`: FELG Software Sp. z o.o., P.IVA 6762479659, VAT EU PL6762479659, KRS 0000520292, Sławkowska 25/9 and ul. Pijarska 9/7, Cracovia. FELG API is genuinely documented (`api.felg.app/docs/` serves a Swagger UI) but gated and priced: START/FLEX/MAX only, 12 €/month in the euro zone, 1000 requests included, 0,01 € each after, max 100 records per request. FAQ states all modules are in every plan, while the listino prices RX 49 €, VoIP 199 €, SMS packs 24-180 € and extra storage from 9 €/month: both are published, carry both. Exit terms are published and decent: 30 days to download patient data from the account after termination, though **no export format is named anywhere**, and importing files out needs the "FELG Box" gateway, unpriced. **Where patient data physically sits is published nowhere** (no country, no region, no data centre, no hosting provider on any page consulted), ISO 27001 is claimed with no certificate number, and the Italian *Informativa sulla privacy* is founded on the Polish pre-GDPR statute ("legge del 29 agosto 1997") naming the "Ispettore Generale per la Protezione dei Dati Personali", the authority replaced in 2018, while the home page advertises GDPR and the FAQ cites NIS 2. Contract and invoicing are Polish: fattura NP with reverse charge, Italian clinic self-accounts IVA.** |

Italy, cloud, dental, **OPEN S.R.L., P.IVA 12179040964**, "realizzato in Italia". **The only row in this section with a genuinely free tier published on its own page, and that is what the post is about: Piano Standard €0/mese, capped at 30 pazienti and mono-utente; Piano Professional €35/mese; Piano Business €25/mese per utente from 2 users, all "esclusi di IVA" and billed "annuale anticipata" after the trial.** A 30-patient cap is not a free product for a working studio and saying exactly where the cap bites is the whole comparison, made in their own numbers and without an adjective. **This row also belongs in the `software-gestionale-dentisti-gratis` post**, the way DASI does for Spain, and that post should be revisited the run this one is written. Settle from their own pages: whether Sistema Tessera Sanitaria and fatturazione elettronica are in the free tier or only above it |

Italy, dental, **desktop Windows with local data storage**, **P.IVA 05975660878**. **Publishes prices and they are perpetual, not monthly: €79 base "senza abbonamento", €99,00, €119,00 and €129,00 for the higher versions, "licenza perpetua", "pagamento unico", "nessun rinnovo automatico della licenza".** This is the clearest licence-shape comparison in any market queue — a one-off €79 against a subscription — and the post is worth writing precisely because the honest answer for a single-chair studio that wants no recurring bill may well be theirs. **Never annualise their figure or set it against a monthly one in the same table cell**; put licence model in its own row as a fact, not a chip, per *Making a post look designed*. Settle from their pages what a perpetual licence excludes: Sistema Tessera Sanitaria filing, fatturazione elettronica via SDI, updates when a tracciato changes, backups and multi-postazione are the questions a desktop perpetual product has to answer in Italy |

Italy, dental, **desktop Windows only** ("il software lavora solo su sistemi operativi Windows", and on a Mac "occorre necessariamente un emulatore di Windows"), **Caes Software s.r.l., P.IVA IT08298111009**, product line dated "dal 1991 ad oggi" with the site copyright running from 1989, both rows they win. **Checked 27 September 2026: the four-level listino is confirmed and the row was short by two levels. Full tariff: Visualizzatore con Agenda € 98,00/anno, BASIC (3 moduli a scelta) € 197,00/anno, PREMIUM (5 moduli a scelta) € 257,00/anno, TOP (tutti i moduli) € 317,00/anno, each paid level plus a € 93,00 first-year activation only, with monthly card renewal at € 8,90 / € 18,10 / € 23,60 / € 29,10. **All of it is "IVA 22% INCLUSA", which nearly every other Italian row quotes net, so never put these figures next to a competitor's without saying so.** Four more things the row did not know. **The multiplier is the per-workstation licence**, published twice: "In caso di uso contemporaneo su più computer è necessario sottoscrivere più abbonamenti" on the listino and "Ogni computer su cui è utilizzato un prodotto della Caes Software deve avere una sua licenza" in the FAQ; whether the optional services are per studio or per postazione is published nowhere, and the post says so rather than computing it. **Sistema TS is a paid annual add-on at € 93,00/anno**, and the SDI service runs € 61,00 (XML only) to € 485,00/anno (unlimited sends), with Caes itself "un'azienda accredita per la trasmissione presso l'Agenzia delle Entrate"; ricette bianche/certificati INPS € 122,00-549,00 by prescriber count, WhatsApp Desktop € 96,00/anno, Modulistica PRO € 146,00/anno, UDI € 73,00/anno, SMS packs € 77,00-976,00 with no expiry, tessera sanitaria readers € 122,00/€ 183,00. Because BASIC gives only three of the selectable modules, a studio wanting agenda, cartella odontoiatrica, contabile, immagini and parodontale is on PREMIUM by design: the published post works that to € 628,00 first year and € 535,00 on renewal for one postazione with Sistema TS and 250 e-invoices, labelled as arithmetic on their figures. **They publish two versions of what happens at expiry** and the post carries both: "il software restrà in sola lettura per un ulteriore anno" on the Horizon page against "la lettura dei dati inseriti è sempre possibile" in the FAQ. Two figures on the page are **commented out of the HTML and must not be cited**: the Magazzino module (sold separately as Stack) and a € 610,00 CRM integration; that is why their prose says "nove moduli" while eight are listed. Rows they win outright: Sistema TS, SDI transmission, ricette bianche elettroniche, UDI register with passaporto implantare, tessera sanitaria reader, Asterisk centralino integration, Italian interface, phone support by their own technicians, no server to run, and quarterly or half-yearly instalments "senza nessun costo aggiuntivo". Their cartella parodontale is real, not a facade (tasche, recessioni, forcazioni, mobilità, indice di placca, sanguinamento with graphs), so **do not reuse the "parodontogramma missing" angle** from the DrawDent post here. **No install-base figure appears anywhere**: the home page counters for "Anni di Esperienza" and "Clienti" render empty, and the post says that rather than guessing. No cloud or web version of Horizon Blue appears on the pages consulted, and no full-archive export is documented, only riepiloghi "in diversi formati (excel, testo, xml e html)". Caes sells a separate product per specialty (Studio D entry-level dental, Studio V veterinary, Horizon ME aesthetic medicine, Studio Fe, FastCeph, Stack Logic), so the "polispecialistiche" in their own headline is the page heading and not a multi-vertical single product: state it that way**

Checked 28 August 2026 and **not** added: EasyDent (`easydent.it` does
not resolve; the EasyDent that exists is American), and no Italian PMS
named Julia exists (the real Software Dental Julia is Spanish and sits in
A1).

Checked 24 September 2026 and **not** added: **MedicHub**
(`medichub.it/gestionale-odontoiatrico`). It is a real Italian dental
cloud PMS, P.IVA 17916741006, with native Sistema Tessera Sanitaria
integration and invio automatico al MEF, and it ranks well for the
Italian head term — and its own page carries the sentence that keeps it
out: **"Da agosto 2026 MedicHub non è più in vendita."** A product
withdrawn from sale is not a comparison target: the reader choosing today
cannot buy it, and the post would be stale the day it shipped. This is
the case *Where the routine publishes and flags* describes, caught before
a post existed rather than after, which is the whole argument for
verifying a queue row at the vendor's own site the day it is added. **Do
not re-add from an aggregator listing** — several still list it as
current. It comes back only if their own site says it is for sale again.

**Sistema Tessera Sanitaria is the Italian compliance moat.** Every
practice in Italy files patient spending to the STS, and Italian vendors
advertise it as a feature. Establish what Dentalpin does and does not do
there before the first Italian comparison goes out, and say it plainly
either way. It belongs in Queue B as its own Italian post.

### A8 · Poland — Polish posts rank here

Unblocked 28 August 2026: every row below was reached at the vendor's own
domain that day, which is what the rotation was waiting for. **These are
written in Polish only.** One market quirk decides the shape of these
posts: most Polish vendors publish prices, so the "does not publish
prices" line that carries other markets is mostly unavailable here, and
the comparison has to win on deployment, data ownership and openness
instead. Prices go in złoty.

| Target | Slug | Why |
|---|---|---|

| ProDentis (INFOTEL SOFTWARE) | `dentalpin-vs-prodentis` | `prodentis.com.pl`, verified 28 August 2026. Dental-only, 20+ years, claims 3.600+ clients, ISO 9001 and 27001, integrates e-Recepta and e-Skierowanie, which is the Polish compliance moat to meet head-on. **Publishes module prices (`/pl/Pages/Cennik`)** |
| Medfile | `dentalpin-vs-medfile` | `medfile.pl/program-dla-dentysty`, verified 28 August 2026. Cloud SaaS, multi-vertical medical with a dedicated dental module and landing: state that plainly. **Publishes prices in PLN (`/cennik`)** |
| SmartDental (Software Clinic sp. z o.o.) | `dentalpin-vs-smartdental` | `software-clinic.pl`, verified 28 August 2026. Their own requirements page (`/opis-programu-stomatologicznego/oprogramowanie-stomatologiczne-wymagania-sprzetowe.html`) names "Microsoft Windows (wersja 64-bitowa)" with .NET Desktop Runtime 8.0 and mentions neither Linux nor macOS, so Windows-only is the fact. The published post carries both, sourced separately.** |

It publishes a full tariff (`/cennik/`) billed per *kalendarz*, meaning per specialist schedule: 139 zł netto for the software, 174 zł with the chatbot (promo, regular 209 zł, promo runs to end of December 2026), 559 zł standard and 839 zł premium for phone reception, all net of 23% VAT, plus per-unit SMS, telemedicine and API charges. No parodontogram is named on any product page consulted; the dental depth claim is scoped to that. And **P1 is named**, twice: "Integracja P1" sits in the FULL-versus-EDM table on `/rozwiazania/oprogramowanie/` and is ticked for *both* versions, while `/proces-wdrozenia/` publishes help configuring "Platforma P1"; §8¹ of the licence goes further and describes support for reporting zdarzenia medyczne to SIM with ICD-9 and ICD-10 code suggestions, at the licensee's risk. e-Recepta, e-skierowanie, eWUŚ, NFZ and KSeF appeared on none of the twenty pages consulted, and neither did parodontogram or periodontogram, so the post scopes each of those. Five more things the row did not know. The tariff is **not per user**: it is a tiered total for the whole practice (FULL annual 127, 206, 255, 304, 355, 408, 455, 504, 558, 610, 660, 708 zł brutto for 1 to 12 users, EDM 84 to 480 zł), so "per user per month" above is wrong. The price is published **two ways**: the cennik labels every figure brutto, while §6 of the licence says the Cennik figures are netto with VAT added, a 23 % gap between two of the vendor's own documents. The trial is published two ways as well, 14 days on the home page, the cennik and the licence definition against "Wypróbuj za darmo przez 30 dni" on the backups, migration and diagram pages, and support is too (home page helpdesk "online jak i pod telefonem" against §11 "wyłącznie za pośrednictwem adresu e-mail"). What makes the post is the vendor's own FULL-versus-EDM table: the cheap EDM tier has **no plany leczenia, no statystyki module at all, no pulpit, no animacje 3D, no zadania, no powiadomienia, no poczekalnia and no wyszukiwanie duplikatów**, and its karta pacjenta is annotated "(uproszczona)", so a practice that plans treatment is on FULL by design. The counterweights are theirs too and are all in the licence: fixed one, three or five-year term, non-refundable fees, no refund on downgrade, liability capped at 2× the period's fee, rękojmia excluded, no uptime figure or SLA anywhere, no source code and an auto-terminating licence on any reverse-engineering attempt, and the licensor may assign the contract to a third party without the customer's consent. Exit is good and the post concedes it: 60 days' read and download after termination, confirmed by a protocol whose template is an annex, then deletion after 90 days. No API documentation appears on any page consulted. The honest verdict for a Polish practice reporting to P1 is that they fit better today, and the published post says so |
